# 2026 Washington Primary Historical Results — EA-01 Audit Correction

## At a glance

Certified results. 294 contests, latest source update 2026-09-11T17:37:08+00:00. No forecasts are published for this report; the margins below are counts, not predictions.

### Closest races in the current count

The closest decision in each contest (the winner, or the last advancing spot in a top-two or top-four primary), smallest share first. These are counts, not predictions; precinct committee officer races are omitted here.

### Why forecasts are withheld

Forecasts are switched off for this report (report mode: historical); even a contest that passed every check below would show counts only.

- 294 contest(s): the count time is unknown or more than 48 hours old.
- 294 contest(s): the source's origin is not fully recorded.
- 294 contest(s): there is no estimate of ballots left to count.
- 294 contest(s): this file has no result status (counting, certified, recount) recorded against it.
- 292 contest(s): the office's winning rule has not been reviewed.
- 289 contest(s): the file covers only part of the contest, or its scope is unreviewed.
- 288 contest(s): the contest has not been matched to a reviewed contest list.
- 15 contest(s): the file's counts do not add up.
- 4 contest(s): files disagree about the contest's geography.

## Geography: county and precinct results

County snapshots: 1; precinct snapshots: 1. Every race's per-county votes are in county-breakdown.csv; precinct rows are in the election's areas/ directory.

Reconciliation of the 2026-09-11T10:37 county snapshot: 507 contest-choice totals compared with the statewide Summary Results, 0 mismatches. Precincts against counties: 5,874 comparisons, 0 mismatches; 151 contest-county pairs have county votes but no precinct rows in the state file.

Data-quality finding: the state precinct file has no precinct rows for 151 contest-county pairs in King County; their precinct-level changes cannot be shown from the state file.

County changes: needs two county snapshots; only 2026-09-11T10:37 is available.

Precinct changes: needs two precinct snapshots; only 2026-09-11T10:37 is available.

No county remaining-ballot counts are configured ([[remaining_ballots]] in election.toml), so no county can be assessed as able to change an outcome.

## About this data

Report mode: historical. Forecast target: none. Information time: 2026-10-25T04:27:06+00:00. Election lifecycle: certified. Experimental and uncalibrated. A versioned evidence gate checks lifecycle, source freshness, coverage, provenance, contest identity, rules and remaining-pool basis. No held-out calibration has validated predictive accuracy. Source publication range: 2026-09-11T17:37:08+00:00 to 2026-09-11T17:37:08+00:00; known source-age range: 1042.8 to 1042.8 hours at information time. Unknown source age: 151 contest record(s); unknown count-update time: 298; incomplete/unverified coverage: 0. Configured sources without snapshots: none. Retrieval and report generation times do not advance a source's count clock.

Lifecycle: certified; tabulation stage: unknown. Authority: https://www.sos.wa.gov/about-office/news/2026/secretary-state-certifies-candidates-and-measures-november-general-election; evidence as of 2026-10-25T04:27:06+00:00.

Latest input label 2026-10-14T11:00; 298 source representations of contests; overlapping jurisdictions may appear separately. Individual source dates may be older; this label is not an authoritative current-count time.

Generated: 2026-11-06T15:44:15+00:00. Per-source vintages and coverage findings are listed in analysis.json (source_summary and coverage_summary).

## Method and limits

Experimental conditional probabilities use disjoint preceding history and a separately weighted verified new-ballot batch. Finite beta-binomial tails use normalized log recurrence and an explicit numerical error policy; oversized calculations are withheld. Hand-set sample caps are unvalidated modeling choices.

Remaining pools require contemporaneous official category counts or declared low/base/high sensitivity scenarios, compatible dates and jurisdictions, and explicit contest/pair participation assumptions. Unknown pools remain unestimated; final totals and turnout overruns cannot establish zero outstanding.

The optional 0–100 evidence rubric is a hand-set, unvalidated heuristic. Separate fields expose lifecycle, source age, coverage, provenance, rule support, remaining-pool basis and calibration. Snapshot count is not independent validation, and rubric grades are not correctness probabilities.

When the available file is only a county slice of a multicounty or statewide race, the report marks it noncontrolling and does not treat it as the official outcome forecast.

For statewide XLSX inputs, the ballot denominator is the larger of the reported Ballots Cast value and the auditable sum of valid votes, overvotes, and undervotes. Any adjustment is disclosed on that race.
